           This file contains birth records from the parish
           of Freudental, South Russia for the decade 186x.
           This information was compiled by Ralph Wiseman and
           coworkers September 1996/7 from the St. Petersburg
           Lutheran Evangelical archives published by the LDS.
           Edited by: Marty McMahon 26 Dec 1997.
